	margin-bottom:5px !important;
}

<h2 class="font18 text-brown">ABOUT WISDOM JOBS - TOP JOB PORTALS IN INDIA</h2>
<p>Wisdomjobs.com is one of the best job search sites in India.  It is the first skill assessment based job portal that offers free skill testing to job seekers. With more than 30 million registered candidates, out of whom 14 million are skill-tested, Wisdom Jobs is helping job seekers to make the step up from just being listed to getting shortlisted.</p>
<p>Wisdom Jobs' free skill testing tool - Pragnya Meter - helps you to take an unlimited number of skill tests, on more than 6000 skills, and to showcase your test scores to recruiters along with their profiles. This gives you a better chance of being shortlisted for over 4 lakh job vacancies on the website. In addition to job opportunities, Wisdomjobs.com also provides free online course material, interview questions and practice tests on more than 4000 skills through its online knowledge library - E-University.</p>
<p>Wisdom Jobs is a single stop for your job search, skill testing, skill development and resume writing services. Whatever be the opening (or naukri) that you are looking for, chances are that you would find it on Wisdomjobs.com. Be it IT jobs, Pharma jobs, Bank jobs, fresher jobs, govt. jobs, non voice jobs in Bangalore or BPO jobs in Hyderabad, you can find the latest job vacancies with us.</p>

$("#searchbox").click(function(){
	$("#keywords").blur();
});
function fnpmlink(skillid)
{
if(skillid!='')
{
window.open("https://www.wisdomjobs.com/pragnyameter/pmredirect.php?catid="+skillid, '_blank');
return false;
}
}

function fnopen_recent(rurl)
{
window.open(rurl,"_self");
return false;
}

$("#keywords").change(function() {
$(this).val($(this).val().trim());
$(this).val($(this).val().substring(0,250));
});
$("#location").change(function() {
$(this).val($(this).val().trim());
$(this).val($(this).val().substring(0,50));
});

$('#SearchSubmit').keydown(function(event){
	if(event.keyCode==9){
	$('#close_btn').focus();return false;
	}
});
$('#close_btn').keydown(function(event){
	if(event.keyCode==9){
	$('#keywords').focus();return false;
	}
});

$("#SearchSubmit").click(function() {
var keywords = $("#keywords").val().trim();
var location = $("#location").val().trim();
var walkinkewords=$("#walkinkeywords").val().trim();
var govtkewords=$("#govtkeywords").val().trim();
var eunivkewords=$("#eunivkeywords").val().trim();
var value=$('input[name=search_in]:checked').val();
if(keywords=="" && location==""  && value=='all')
{
$("#mygroup-error").css("display","");
$("#mygroup-error").html("Please Enter Keyword Or Location");
$("#keywords").focus();
return false;
}
if(eunivkewords=="" && value=='euniv')
{
$("#mygroup-error").css("display","");
$("#mygroup-error").html("Please Enter Keyword ");
$("#keywords").focus();
return false;
}
if(walkinkewords==""  && value=='walkin')
{
$("#mygroup-error").css("display","");
$("#mygroup-error").html("Please Enter Keyword");
$("#keywords").focus();
return false;
}
if(govtkewords==""  && value=='govt_jobs')
{
$("#mygroup-error").css("display","");
$("#mygroup-error").html("Please Enter Keyword");
$("#keywords").focus();
return false;
}
}); 

$("#keywords").change(function(e) {
var val = $(this).val();
$(this).val(val.replace(/^\s/g, "").replace(/\s+/g, " "));
});

</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
function fninterestme(str)
{	
	var dataString = "restype="+str;
	$.ajax({
	url: 'https://www.wisdomjobs.com/profiledashboard/ajax_jsinterest.php',
	type: 'POST',
	data:dataString,
	success: function (returndata)
	{
	return returndata;
	}
	});
}

(function(){
  $('.carousel-showmanymoveone .item').each(function(){
    var itemToClone = $(this);

    for (var i=1;i<2;i++) {
      itemToClone = itemToClone.next();

      // wrap around if at end of item collection
      if (!itemToClone.length) {
        itemToClone = $(this).siblings(':first');
      }

      // grab item, clone, add marker class, add to collection
      itemToClone.children(':first-child').clone()
        .addClass("cloneditem-"+(i))
        .appendTo($(this));
    }
  });
}());</script>

else if(sitename=='sg'){window.open('http://www.wisdomjobs.sg/', '_blank');}
else if(sitename=='au'){window.open('http://www.wisdomjobs.com.au/', '_blank');}
else if(sitename=='nz'){window.open('http://www.wisdomjobs.net.nz/', '_blank');}

function myFunction()
{
	
	var value=$('input[name=search_in]:checked').val();
	//alert(value);
	if(value=="govt_jobs")
	{
		$(".location_div").css("display","none");
		$(".date_div").css("display","none");
		$("#walkinkeywords").hide();
		$("#keywords").hide();
		$("#eunivkeywords").hide();
		$("#govtkeywords").show();
		
	}
	else if(value=="euniv")
	{
		$(".location_div").css("display","none");
		$(".date_div").css("display","none");
		$("#walkinkeywords").hide();
		$("#keywords").hide();
		$("#eunivkeywords").show();
		$("#govtkeywords").hide();
		
	}
	else if(value=="walkin")
	{
		$(".location_div").css("display","none");
		$(".date_div").css("display","none");
		$("#govtkeywords").hide();
		$("#walkinkeywords").show();
		$("#keywords").hide();
		$("#eunivkeywords").hide();
 			

		
		
		/*$.ajax({
			url:"walk.json",
			data:{},
			method:"post",
			success:function(data)
			{
				var datalength=data['walkinjob'].length;
				
				var ul="<ul class='ui-autocomplete ui-front ui-menu ui-widget ui-widget-content' id='ul_new' ></ul>";
				$("#keynames").append(ul);
				for(var i=0;i<datalength;i++)
				{
					
						var datas="<li class='ui-menu-item'>"+data['walkinjob'][i]['FuncName']+"</li>";
						
						$('#ul_new').append(datas);
						
						
				}
				        
					
			}
			
		});*/
		
	}
	else{
		$(".location_div").css("display","block");
		$(".date_div").css("display","block");
		$("#govtkeywords").hide();
		$("#eunivkeywords").hide();
		$("#walkinkeywords").hide();$("#keywords").show();
	}
}

$(".jobsrc").click(function(){
	var _keywrd=$("#mkeywords").val();
	if(_keywrd.trim().length>0){
		$("#keywords").val(_keywrd);
		$("#popupsearch").attr("style","position: fixed !important; left: -10000000px !important;");
		$("input[value=all]").click();	
		$("#SearchSubmit").trigger("click");
	}
});

		$("#location").val('');
		$("#keywords").val(_keywrd);
		$("#popupsearch").attr("style","position: fixed !important; left: -10000000px !important;");	
		$("#SearchSubmit").trigger("click");
	}
});

<script type="text/javascript">
	$('#loginsubmitbtn').click(function(){
		var username='';
		var userpwd='';
		var errorcnt=0;
		username=$('#loginusername').val();
		username=$.trim(username);
		userpwd=$('#userloginpassword1').val();
		userpwd=$.trim(userpwd);
		if(userpwd==''){errorcnt++;
			$('#loginpwderror1').html('Please enter your Password');
			$('#userloginpassword1').focus();
		}
		if(userpwd!='' && userpwd.length<6){errorcnt++;
			$('#loginpwderror1').html('Please enter minimum 6 characters');
			$('#userloginpassword1').focus();
		}
		if(userpwd!='' && userpwd.length>39){errorcnt++;
			$('#loginpwderror1').html('Please enter maximum 40 characters');
			$('#userloginpassword1').focus();
		}
		if(username==''){errorcnt++;
			$('#loginunameerror').html('Please enter email');
			$('#loginusername').focus();
		}
		if(username!='' && username.length<6){errorcnt++;
			$('#loginunameerror').html('Please enter minimum 6 characters');
			$('#loginusername').focus();		
		}
		if(username!='' && username.length>50){errorcnt++;
			$('#loginunameerror').html('Please enter maximum 50 characters');
			$('#loginusername').focus();		
		}
		if(errorcnt==0){
			$('#loginsubmitbtn').attr('disabled',true);
			var save_search_option=$('#save_search_option').val();
				$.ajax({
					type:'post',
					url:'https://www.wisdomjobs.com/loginwithpopup.php',
					data:{username:username,userpwd:userpwd},
					success:function(response){if(response=='SUCCESS'){ if(save_search_option==1){ window.location='https://www.wisdomjobs.com/profiledashboard/manage-savesearch.php'; }else{ window.location='https://www.wisdomjobs.com/profiledashboard/dashboard.php';}  } if(response=='FAILC'){ $('#loginserverError').html("The Email and password you entered don't match"); } if(response=='FAILD'){ $('#loginserverError').html("UserData Not Found");  } if(response=='FAILR'){ window.location='https://www.wisdomjobs.com/registerform.html'; } $('#loginsubmitbtn').attr('disabled',false);},error:function(error){ console.log(error); }
				});
			$('.loginsubmitbtn').attr('disabled',false);
		}
		return false;
	});
	$('#loginusername').on('keyup',function(){
		$('#loginserverError').html('');
		var username=$(this).val();
		username=$.trim(username);
		if(username==''){
			$('#loginunameerror').html('Please enter email');
			$('#loginusername').focus();
		}
		if(username!='' && username.length<6){
			$('#loginunameerror').html('Please enter minimum 6 characters');
			$('#loginusername').focus();		
		}
		if(username!='' && username.length>50){
			$('#loginunameerror').html('Please enter maximum 50 characters');
			$('#loginusername').focus();		
		}
		if(username!='' && username.length>5 && username.length<50){
			$('#loginunameerror').html('');
		}
	});
	$('#loginusername').blur(function(){
		var username=$(this).val();
		if(username==''){
			$('#loginunameerror').html('Please enter email');
		}
	});
	$('#userloginemail').keyup(function(){
		$('#loginserverError').html('');
		var username=$(this).val();
		username=$.trim(username);
		if(username==''){
			$('#loginemailiderror').html('Please enter your Email ID');
			$('#userloginemail').focus();
		}
		if(username!=''){
			
			var isValid = /^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+@[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+\.([a-zA-Z]{2,4})$/.test(username) ? true : false;
			if(isValid==false){
				$('#loginemailiderror').html('Please enter valid Email ID');
				$('#userloginemail').focus();
			}
			else
			{
				$('#loginemailiderror').html('');
			}
		}
	});
	$('#userloginpassword').keyup(function(){
		$('#loginserverError').html('');
		var userpwd=$(this).val();
		userpwd=$.trim(userpwd);
		if(userpwd==''){
			$('#loginpwderror').html('Please enter your Password');
			$('#userloginpassword').focus();
		}
		if(userpwd!='' && userpwd.length<6){
			$('#loginpwderror').html('Please enter minimum 6 characters');
			$('#userloginpassword').focus();
		}
		if(userpwd!='' && userpwd.length>39){
			$('#loginpwderror').html('Please enter maximum 40 characters');
			$('#userloginpassword').focus();
		}
		if(userpwd!='' && userpwd.length>5 && userpwd.length<39){
			$('#loginpwderror').html('');
		}
	});
	$('#userloginpassword1').keyup(function(){
		$('#loginserverError').html('');
		var userpwd=$(this).val();
		userpwd=$.trim(userpwd);
		if(userpwd==''){
			$('#loginpwderror1').html('Please enter your Password');
			$('#userloginpassword1').focus();
		}
		if(userpwd!='' && userpwd.length<6){
			$('#loginpwderror1').html('Please enter minimum 6 characters');
			$('#userloginpassword1').focus();
		}
		if(userpwd!='' && userpwd.length>39){
			$('#loginpwderror1').html('Please enter maximum 40 characters');
			$('#userloginpassword1').focus();
		}
		if(userpwd!='' && userpwd.length>5 && userpwd.length<39){
			$('#loginpwderror1').html('');
		}
	});
	function openlogincontent(event){
		$('#loginunameerror').html('');
		$('#loginpwderror1').html('');
		$('#loginserverError').html('');
		$('#loginusername').val('');
		$('#userloginpassword1').val('');
		$('#loginwithpopup').click();
		event.preventDefault();
	}

		var jobrec=$(this).attr('class');
		if(jobrec>0){
		var notifications=$('.notif-count').html();
		var mnotifications=parseInt(notifications)-parseInt(jobrec);
		if(parseInt(mnotifications)==0){ $('.notif-count').hide();}
		if(parseInt(mnotifications)>0){ $('.notif-count').html(mnotifications);}
		document.jobreccnt.submit();
		}
	});
